a spec or role is a few KiB, and one frame holds all three members\012+SIGNED_TEXT_MAX = 64 * 1024\012+_AGENT_ID = re.compile(r"ag-[0-9a-f]{32}")\012+_HEX64 = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")\012+\012+\012+class SignedRoleRefused(ValueError):\012+    """The evidence does not derive the signed role; ``name`` says which link of the chain broke."""\012+\012+    def __init__(self, name: str, detail: str = "") -> None:\012+        self.name, self.detail = name, detail\012+        super().__init__(f"{name}: {detail}" if detail else name)\012+\012+\012+@dataclass(frozen=True)\012+class SignedRole:\012+    """The canonical spec text and role text of an occurrence, with its agent id: what :func:`signed_withhold` re-hashes."""\012+    agent_id: str\012+    spec: str\012+    role: str\012+\012+    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\012+        if type(self.agent_id) is not str or _AGENT_ID.fullmatch(self.agent_id) is None:\012+            raise ValueError("signed.agent_id is not ag-<32 hex>")\012+        for name in ("spec", "role"):\012+            v = getattr(self, name)\012+            if type(v) is not str or not v or len(v.encode("utf-8", "replace")) > SIGNED_TEXT_MAX:\012+                raise ValueError(f"signed.{name} is not a short text")\012+\012+\012+def _json(text: str, what: str) -> dict:\012+    try:\012+        obj = json.loads(text)\012+    except (ValueError, RecursionError):\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused(f"{what}-unreadable", "not JSON") from None\012+    if type(obj) is not dict:\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused(f"{what}-unreadable", "not an object")\012+    try:\012+        canonical_form = _canonical(obj)\012+    except (ValueError, TypeError, RecursionError):\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused(f"{what}-unreadable", "not canonical-able") from None\012+    if canonical_form != text:\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused(f"{what}-not-canonical", "the text is not its own canonical form")\012+    return obj\012+\012+\012+def signed_withhold(signed: SignedRole, spec_hash: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:\012+    """The sorted withhold patterns of the role ``signed`` proves, bound to ``spec_hash`` (the hash the verified link\012+    names). Raises :class:`SignedRoleRefused` at the first broken link: the spec's hash (``spec-hash``), the role's\012+    (``role-hash``), a text that is not its canonical form, or a withhold that is not a list of strings. ``spec_hash``\012+    None compares nothing against a link (the operator-uid side, whose store already re-hashed the spec to its\012+    occurrence's hash); the server always passes the verified link's."""\012+    if spec_hash is not None and (type(spec_hash) is not str or _HEX64.fullmatch(spec_hash) is None):\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused("spec-hash", "the link names no spec hash")\012+    spec = _json(signed.spec, "spec")\012+    got = hashlib.sha256(SPEC_HASH_PREFIX + _canonical({"agent_id": signed.agent_id, "spec": spec}).encode()).hexdigest()\012+    if spec_hash is not None and got != spec_hash:\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused("spec-hash", "the spec text is not the one the link names")\012+    pin = spec.get("role")\012+    role_hash = pin.get("role_hash") if type(pin) is dict else None\012+    if type(role_hash) is not str or _HEX64.fullmatch(role_hash) is None:\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused("role-hash", "the spec pins no role hash")\012+    if hashlib.sha256(ROLE_HASH_PREFIX + signed.role.encode("utf-8", "strict")).hexdigest() != role_hash:\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused("role-hash", "the role text is not the one the spec pins")\012+    role = _json(signed.role, "role")\012+    ceiling = role.get("ceiling")\012+    withhold: Any = ceiling.get("withhold", []) if type(ceiling) is dict else []\012+    if type(withhold) is not list or any(type(p) is not str for p in withhold):\012+        raise SignedRoleRefused("withhold", "the role's ceiling.withhold is not a list of patterns")\012+    return tuple(sorted(withhold))\012+\012+\012+def withhold_problem(signed: tuple[str, ...], launched: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, str] | None:\012+    """``(name, detail)`` when the withhold a launch (or a manifest) carries is not the signed role's, else None. Both\012+    uids call this one function. A signed pattern missing from ``launched`` is a LOOSENING (``withhold-loosened``:\012+    the snapshot would carry a path the role withholds); a pattern the role never declared is ``withhold-mismatch``\012+    (more withheld than he signed is not loosening, but it is not the snapshot his signature names, and the digest the\012+    run records would no longer follow from the role)."""\012+    have, want = set(launched), set(signed)\012+    if want - have:\012+        return "withhold-loosened", f"{len(want - have)} of the signed role's withhold patterns are not in the snapshot's"\012+    if have - want:\012+        return "withhold-mismatch", f"{len(have - So the read\012+token lives HOST-side, in ``<levain home>/seat_gh/<owner>`` (0600 in a 0700 directory, the seat profile denies the seat\012+that directory exactly as it denies the launcher key directory), and the seat reaches it only through the stdio MCP\012+server ``levain_gh`` (:mod:`levain.seat_gh_mcp`), which runs outside the sandbox and calls the two verbs here:\012+\012+* :func:`gh_read`: ``GET`` only, to ``api.github.com``, on a path of the fixed allowlist (:data:`REPOS` x\012+  :data:`READ_SEGMENTS`). No redirect is followed. The token goes in one ``Authorization`` header, never in argv, never\012+  in an environment, and the returned text is scrubbed of it.\012+* :func:`git_fetch`: the host's git fetches the allowed repository's branches into a Levain-owned bare mirror\012+  (``<home>/gh_mirror/<owner>/<name>.git``); the seat then fetches from that LOCAL path. The git child's environment is\012+  built from scratch (a fresh empty HOME, no global or system config, no ``GIT_*`` from the caller), the credential\012+  reaches git through a per-URL credential helper that answers only ``github.com`` and only the allowed repository's\012+  ``.git`` path, and no remote transport but ``https`` is allowed.\012+\012+Two tokens, not one: a fine-grained PAT has ONE resource owner and ``levainhq/levain`` and ``phillipclapham/flow`` have\012+different owners (Z2B_FRAME section 2). An absent, expired or unreadable token is ONE fixed refusal sentence and the\012+verb stops: there is no fallback to ``gh``'s hosts.yml, the keychain, ``GH_TOKEN`` in the environment or an ssh agent.\012+Every refusal is fixed words (the seat reads them and learns nothing about the token store).\012+\012+Every call leaves one line in ``<home>/push/seat_gh.jsonl`` (verb, repository, outcome, status); it never holds a token.\012+\012+Known edges (one line each): the token's own expiry is the one the operator recorded (or GitHub's response header gave)\012+and is enforced here, but GitHub revokes a token on its side first when the operator deletes it; a mirror fetch that is\012+killed mid-way leaves a half-updated mirror that the next fetch repairs (git's own atomic ref updates).\012+"""\012+from __future__ import annotations\012+\012+import base64\012+import getpass\012+import hashlib\012+import http.client\012+import ipaddress\012+import json\012+import os\012+import re\012+import shlex\012+import stat\012+import subprocess\012+import sys\012+import tempfile\012+import time\012+from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence\012+from datetime import datetime, timezone\012+from pathlib import Path\012+from typing import Any\012+from urllib.parse import quote, urlencode, urlsplit\012+\012+from levain.consent import push\012+\012+__all__ = ["REPOS", "OWNERS", "READ_SEGMENTS", "ACTIONS_CHILDREN", "QUERY_KEYS", "API_HOST", "API_BASE", "GIT_BASE", "TOKEN_DIR", "MIRROR_DIR",\012+           "LOG_NAME", "GhRefused", "path_refusal", "query_refusal", "scrub", "token_fingerprint", "set_token",\012+           "clear_token", "token_status", "gh_read", "git_fetch", "credential_helper", "main"]\012+\012+#: The allowed repositories and the resource owner whose token reads each. A code constant: no argument names another.\012+REPOS: Mapping[str, str] = {"levainhq/levain": "levainhq", "phillipclapham/flow": "phillipclapham"}\012+OWNERS = tuple(sorted(set(REPOS.values())))\012+#: The path segment right after ``/repos/<owner>/<repo>`` a seat may read (or none, the repository itself).\012+READ_SEGMENTS = frozenset({"contents", "pulls", "issues", "actions", "commits", "branches"})\012+#: The query names a seat may send, and what a value may be made of.\012+#: ``actions`` also carries the secrets, variables and runner registrations of a repository: only these children read.\012+ACTIONS_CHILDREN = frozenset({"runs", "jobs", "workflows", "artifacts"})\012+QUERY_KEYS = frozenset({"ref", "per_page", "page", "state", "sha", "path", "branch", "event", "status", "since",\012+                        "until", "head", "base", "sort", "direction"})\012+_QUERY_VALUE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.@/~+:,=-]{0,200}")\012+_QUERY_MAX = 10\012+API_HOST = "api.github.com"\012+API_BASE = f"https://{API_HOST}"\012+GIT_HOST = "github.com"\012+GIT_BASE = f"https://{GIT_HOST}/"\012+TOKEN_DIR = "seat_gh"\012+#: NOT inside :data:`TOKEN_DIR`: the seat is denied that directory and must read the mirror.\012+MIRROR_DIR = "gh_mirror"\012+LOG_NAME = "seat_gh.jsonl"\012+CRED_USER = "x-access-token"\012+MAX_BODY = 200_000\012+HTTP_TIMEOUT = 30\012+FETCH_TIMEOUT = 600\012+EXPIRY_HEADER = "github-authentication-token-expiration"\012+\012+# fixed-words refusals: the whole text a seat ever reads about a refusal\012+R_METHOD = "levain_gh refuses: only GET."\012+R_PATH = "levain_gh refuses: that path is not a read path of an allowed repository."\012+R_REPO = "levain_gh refuses: that repository is not one a seat may read."\012+R_QUERY = "levain_gh refuses: that query is not an allowed one."\012+R_HOST = "levain_gh refuses: that host is not GitHub's API."\012+R_REDIRECT = "levain_gh refuses: GitHub answered with a redirect; none is followed."\012+R_TOKEN_ABSENT = "levain_gh refuses: no read token is installed for that repository's owner."\012+R_TOKEN_EXPIRED = "levain_gh refuses: the read token for that repository's owner has expired."\012+R_TOKEN_BAD = "levain_gh refuses: the read token for that repository's owner cannot be used."\012+R_TRANSPORT = "levain_gh could not reach GitHub."\012+R_FETCH = "levain_gh: the fetch into the mirror failed."\012+\012+_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,255}")\012+# the shapes GitHub issues, scrubbed from anything returned even when it is not the token on file\012+_TOKEN_SHAPE = re.compile(r"(?:gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,})")\012+_PATH = re.compile(r"/repos/([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)((?:/[A-Za-z0-9_.@~+=,-]+)*)")\012+\012+\012+class GhRefused(Exception):\012+    """A verb is refused. ``str()`` is a fixed sentence (plus, for a failed fetch, a scrubbed git detail)."""\012+\012+\012+# --- the judges: pure, no I/O -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def path_refusal(path: Any) -> str | None:\012+    """None when ``path`` is ``/repos/<allowed repo>`` or ``/repos/<allowed repo>/<read segment>[/...]``, else the fixed\012+    refusal. The path alphabet has no ``%``, ``?``, ``#``, ``\\`` or space, so no encoded traversal or second query\012+    reaches the request; ``.`` and ``..`` as a segment are refused whatever follows."""\012+    if not isinstance(path, str) or len(path) > 1024:\012+        return R_PATH\012+    m = _PATH.fullmatch(path)\012+    if not m:\012+        return R_PATH\012+    repo, rest = m.groups()\012+    if repo not in REPOS:\012+        return R_REPO\012+    segs = rest.split("/")[1:] if rest else []\012+    if any(s in (".", "..") for s in segs):\012+        return R_PATH\012+    if segs and segs[0] not in READ_SEGMENTS:\012+        return R_PATH\012+    if segs[:1] == ["actions"] and len(segs) > 1 and segs[1] not in ACTIONS_CHILDREN:\012+        return R_PATH\012+    return None\012+\012+\012+def query_refusal(query: Any) -> str | None:\012+    if query is None:\012+        return None\012+    if not isinstance(query, Mapping) or len(query) > _QUERY_MAX:\012+        return R_QUERY\012+    for k, v in query.items():\012+        if k not in QUERY_KEYS or not isinstance(v, (str, int)) or isinstance(v, bool):\012+            return R_QUERY\012+        if not _QUERY_VALUE.fullmatch(str(v)):\012+            return R_QUERY\012+    return None\012+\012+\012+def scrub(text: str, tokens: Sequence[str] = ()) -> str:\012+    """``text`` with every given token (raw, url-quoted, and as the base64 of the basic-auth pairs) and every\012+    GitHub-token-shaped run replaced by ``[token]``."""\012+    for t in tokens:\012+        if not t:\012+            continue\012+        forms = {t, quote(t, safe=""), base64.b64encode(t.encode()).decode(),\012+                 base64.b64encode(f"{CRED_USER}:{t}".encode()).decode()}\012+        for f in sorted(forms, key=len, reverse=True):\012+            text = text.replace(f, "[token]")\012+    return _TOKEN_SHAPE.sub("[token]", text)\012+\012+\012+def token_fingerprint(token: str) -> str:\012+    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]\012+\012+\012+# --- the token store --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def token_dir(home: Path | str) -> Path:\012+    return Path(home) / TOKEN_DIR\012+\012+\012+def _now() -> datetime:\012+    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\012+\012+\012+def _parse_expiry(text: str) -> datetime:\012+    """``2026-12-01``, ``2026-12-01T00:00:00Z`` or GitHub's ``2026-12-01 00:00:00 UTC``; a date is its first instant (UTC),\012+    the conservative reading."""\012+    s = text.strip()\012+    if s.endswith(" UTC"):\012+        s = s[:-4] + "+00:00"\012+    s = s.replace("Z", "+00:00").replace(" ", "T", 1)\012+    d = datetime.fromisoformat(s)\012+    return d if d.tzinfo else d.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\012+\012+\012+def _private_dir(path: Path) -> None:\012+    try:\012+        os.mkdir(path, 0o700)\012+    except FileExistsError:\012+        pass\012+    st = os.lstat(path)\012+    if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD)\012+    if stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) != 0o700:\012+        os.chmod(path, 0o700)\012+\012+\012+def _read_record(home: Path | str, owner: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\012+    """The owner's token record, or :class:`GhRefused`. The directory and the file must be this user's own, not links,\012+    and closed to group and world: anything else is refused, never repaired (a loosened store is not trusted)."""\012+    d = token_dir(home)\012+    f = d / owner\012+    try:\012+        dst = os.lstat(d)\012+        if not stat.S_ISDIR(dst.st_mode) or dst.st_uid != os.getuid() or dst.st_mode & 0o077:\012+            raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD)\012+        fst = os.lstat(f)\012+    except FileNotFoundError:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_ABSENT) from None\012+    except OSError:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD) from None\012+    if not stat.S_ISREG(fst.st_mode) or fst.st_uid != os.getuid() or fst.st_mode & 0o077:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD)\012+    try:\012+        fd = os.open(f, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)\012+        with os.fdopen(fd, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:\012+            rec = json.loads(fh.read(4096))\012+    except (OSError, ValueError):\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD) from None\012+    if not (isinstance(rec, dict) and rec.get("owner") == owner and isinstance(rec.get("token"), str)\012+            and _TOKEN_RE.fullmatch(rec["token"]) and isinstance(rec.get("expires_at"), str)):\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD)\012+    return rec\012+\012+\012+def _live_token(home: Path | str, owner: str, now: datetime) -> str:\012+    rec = _read_record(home, owner)\012+    try:\012+        expires = _parse_expiry(rec["expires_at"])\012+    except ValueError:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_BAD) from None\012+    if now >= expires:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TOKEN_EXPIRED)\012+    return rec["token"]\012+\012+\012+def _write_record(home: Path | str, owner: str, token: str, expires: datetime) -> None:\012+    d = token_dir(home)\012+    Path(home).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\012+    _private_dir(d)\012+    rec = {"v": 1, "owner": owner, "fingerprint": token_fingerprint(token), "expires_at": expires.isoformat(),\012+           "token": token}\012+    tmp = d / f".{owner}.{os.getpid()}"\012+    fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)\012+    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:\012+        fh.write(json.dumps(rec, sort_keys=True) + "\n")\012+    os.replace(tmp, d / owner)\012+\012+\012+def clear_token(home: Path | str, owner: str) -> bool:\012+    """Delete the owner's token file; True when one was there. The next verb for that owner refuses."""\012+    if owner not in OWNERS:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_REPO)\012+    try:\012+        os.unlink(token_dir(home) / owner)\012+    except FileNotFoundError:\012+        return False\012+    return True\012+\012+\012+def token_status(home: Path | str, owner: str, now: datetime | None = None) -> str:\012+    """One line for the operator: absent / unusable / expired / live, with the fingerprint and expiry, never the token."""\012+    if owner not in OWNERS:\012+        raise GhRefused(R_REPO)\012+    try:\012+        rec = _read_record(home, owner)\012+    except GhRefused as exc:\012+        return f"{owner}: " + ("no token installed" if str(exc) == R_TOKEN_ABSENT else "token unusable (not private, or damaged)")\012+    try:\012+        live = (now or _now()) < _parse_expiry(rec["expires_at"])\012+    except ValueError:\012+        return f"{owner}: token unusable (expiry unreadable)"\012+    return f"{owner}: {'live' if live else 'EXPIRED'}  fingerprint {rec.get('fingerprint')}  expires {rec['expires_at']}"\012+\012+\012+# --- the HTTP read ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def _connection(base_url: str) -> http.client.HTTPConnection:\012+    """A connection to GitHub's API. ``https`` only to :data:`API_HOST`; plain ``http`` only to a loopback address (a\012+    test's local server): the token never travels in the clear to anything else, whatever ``base_url`` says."""\012+    u = urlsplit(base_url)\012+    host = u.hostname or ""\012+    if u.scheme == "https" and host == API_HOST and u.port in (None, 443) and not u.path.strip("/"):\012+        return http.client.HTTPSConnection(host, 443, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT)\012+    if u.scheme == "http" and not u.path.strip("/"):\012+        try:\012+            loop = ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback\012+        except ValueError:\012+            loop = False\012+        if loop:\012+            return http.client.HTTPConnection(host, u.port or 80, timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT)\012+    raise GhRefused(R_HOST)\012+\012+\012+def _get(base_url: str, target: str, token: str) -> tuple[int, dict[str, str], str]:\012+    conn = _connection(base_url)\012+    try:\012+        conn.request("GET", target, headers={\012+            "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",\012+            "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28", "User-Agent": "levain-seat-gh"})\012+        resp = conn.getresponse()\012+        status = resp.status\012+        if 300 <= status < 400:\012+            raise GhRefused(R_REDIRECT)\012+        raw = resp.read(MAX_BODY + 1)\012+        headers = {k.lower(): v for k, v in resp.getheaders()}\012+    except GhRefused:\012+        raise\012+    except (OSError, http.client.HTTPException):\012+        raise GhRefused(R_TRANSPORT) from None\012+    finally:\012+        conn.close()\012+    return status, headers, raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")\012+\012+\012+def _log(home: Path | str, row: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\012+    """One line in ``<home>/push/seat_gh.jsonl``; never a token (rows are built from fixed fields only). A record that\012+    cannot be written is dropped, never raised."""\012+    try:\012+        push._private_dir(Path(home) / "push")\012+        push.append_line(Path(home) / "push" / LOG_NAME, {**row, "at": time.time()})\012+    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\012+        pass\012+\012+\012+def gh_read(home: Path | str, path: Any, query: Any = None, *, method: str = "GET", api_base: str = API_BASE,\012+            now: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, str]:\012+    """GET ``path`` (+ ``query``) from GitHub's API as the path's owner. Returns ``(status, scrubbed body)``; raises\012+    :class:`GhRefused` (fixed words) for anything outside the allowlist or a token that is absent or expired."""\012+    base: dict[str, Any] = {"verb": "gh_read", "path": path if isinstance(path, str) else None}\012+    try:\012+        if method != "GET":\012+            raise GhRefused(R_METHOD)\012+        problem = path_refusal(path) or query_refusal(query)\012+        if problem:\012+            raise GhRefused(problem)\012+        repo = _PATH.fullmatch(path).group(1)               # type: ignore[union-attr]  # path_refusal vouched for it\012+        token = _live_token(home, REPOS[repo], now or _now())\012+        target = path + (("?" + urlencode(sorted((str(k), str(v)) for k, v in query.items()))) if query else "")\012+        status, _headers, body = _get(api_base, target, token)\012+        body = scrub(body[:MAX_BODY], (token,))\012+        _log(home, {**base, "outcome": "read", "status": status})\012+        return status, body\012+    except GhRefused as exc:\012+        _log(home, {**base, "outcome": "refused", "detail": str(exc)[:200]})\012+        raise\012+\012+\012+# --- the git fetch ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def mirror_path(home: Path | str, repo: str) -> Path:\012+    owner, name = repo.split("/")\012+    return Path(home) / MIRROR_DIR / owner / f"{name}.git"\012+\012+\012+def _helper_command(python: str, home: Path | str) -> str:\012+    return "!" + " ".join([shlex.quote(python), "-EP", "-B", "-m", "levain.seat_gh", "credential-helper",\012+                           "--home", shlex.quote(str(home))])\012+\012+\012+def _fetch_argv(git: str, mirror: Path, url: str, helper: str) -> list[str]:\012+    return [*push.git_isolation_args(git, str(mirror),\012+                                     extra_config=("gc.auto=0", "credential.helper=",\012+                                                   f"credential.{GIT_BASE.rstrip('/')}.helper={helper}",\012+                                                   "credential.useHttpPath=true", "http.followRedirects=false",\012+                                                   "protocol.allow=never", "protocol.https.allow=always",\012+                                                   "protocol.file.allow=always")),\012+            "fetch", "--quiet", "--prune", "--no-tags", "--no-write-fetch-head", url, "+refs/heads/*:refs/heads/*"]\012+\012+\012+def _git_env(home_dir: str, scheme: str) -> dict[str, str]:\012+    """Rebuilt from scratch, nothing inherited: a fresh empty HOME, no global or system config, no prompt, one transport."""\012+    return {"PATH": push.TRUSTED_PATH, "HOME": home_dir, "LANG": "C", "GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL": "/dev/null",\012+            "GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM": "1", "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0", "GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH": "1",\012+            "GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS": "1", "GIT_ALLOW_PROTOCOL": scheme}\012+\012+\012+def _ensure_mirror(git: str, home: Path | str, repo: str, env: dict[str, str]) -> Path:\012+    m = mirror_path(home, repo)\012+    Path(home).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\012+    _private_dir(Path(home) / MIRROR_DIR)\012+    _private_dir(m.parent)\012+    if not os.path.lexists(m):\012+        r = subprocess.run([git, "init", "--quiet", "--bare", "--template=", str(m)], env=env, cwd="/",\012+                           stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\012+        if r.returncode:\012+            raise GhRefused(R_FETCH)\012+    _private_dir(m)\012+    hooks = m / "hooks"\012+    if os.path.lexists(hooks):\012+        import shutil\012+        if os.path.islink(hooks) or not os.path.isdir(hooks):\012+            os.unlink(hooks)\012+        else:\012+            shutil.rmtree(hooks)\012+    tmp = m / "config.levain-tmp"\012+    fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)\012+    with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as fh:\012+        fh.write(push.MIRROR_CONFIG)\012+    os.replace(tmp, m / "config")\012+    return m\012+\012+\012+def git_fetch(home: Path | str, repo: Any, *, git_base: str = GIT_BASE, python: str | None = None,\012+              now: datetime | None = None) -> str:\012+    """Fetch every branch of the allowed repository ``repo`` into its Levain-owned bare mirror as the repository's\012+    owner, and return the mirror path and its branch tips (scrubbed). The token is checked FIRST: an absent or expired one\012+    refuses before any git runs."""\012+    base: dict[str, Any] = {"verb": "git_fetch", "repo": repo if isinstance(repo, str) else None}\012+    try:\012+        if not isinstance(repo, str) or repo not in REPOS:\012+            raise GhRefused(R_REPO)\012+        token = _live_token(home, REPOS[repo], now or _now())\012+        scheme = urlsplit(git_base).scheme\012+        if scheme not in ("https", "file") or not git_base.endswith("/"):\012+            raise GhRefused(R_HOST)\012+        git = push.git_bin()\012+        url = f"{git_base}{repo}.git"\012+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="levain-gh-") as scratch:\012+            env = _git_env(scratch, scheme)\012+            mirror = _ensure_mirror(git, home, repo, env)\012+            argv = _fetch_argv(git, mirror, url, _helper_command(python or sys.executable, home))\012+            try:\012+                r = subprocess.run(argv, env=env, cwd="/", stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True, text=True,\012+                                   encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=FETCH_TIMEOUT)\012+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\012+                raise GhRefused(R_FETCH) from None\012+            if r.returncode:\012+                detail = scrub((r.stderr or "").strip()[-300:], (token,))\012+                raise GhRefused(f"{R_FETCH} {detail}".strip())\012+            refs = subprocess.run(push.git_isolation_args(git, str(mirror)) + [\012+                "for-each-ref", "--count=200", "--format=%(objectname:short) %(refname:short)", "refs/heads"],\012+                env=env, cwd="/", stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\012+        out = scrub(f"mirror: {mirror}\n{refs.stdout}", (token,))\012+        _log(home, {**base, "outcome": "fetched"})\012+        return out\012+    except GhRefused as exc:\012+        _log(home, {**base, "outcome": "refused", "detail": str(exc)[:200]})\012+        raise\012+\012+\012+# --- the credential helper (git's, run by the host's fetch) -------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def credential_helper(action: str, stdin_text: str, home: Path | str, out: Any, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> int:\012+    """git's credential-helper protocol, ``get`` only. It answers when the request is ``https``, ``github.com`` and the\012+    path is exactly an allowed repository's ``.git``; for anything else it prints nothing (git then has no credential and\012+    the prompt is disabled). The token reaches git's stdin pipe and nothing else."""\012+    if action != "get":\012+        return 0\012+    req: dict[str, str] = {}\012+    for line in stdin_text.splitlines():\012+        k, sep, v = line.partition("=")\012+        if sep:\012+            req[k] = v\012+    path = req.get("path", "")\012+    repo = path[:-4] if path.endswith(".git") else ""\012+    if req.get("protocol") != "https" or req.get("host") != GIT_HOST or repo not in REPOS:\012+        return 0\012+    try:\012+        token = _live_token(home, REPOS[repo], now or _now())\012+    except GhRefused:\012+        return 0\012+    out.write(f"username={CRED_USER}\npassword={token}\n")\012+    return 0\012+\012+\012+# --- the operator's token commands -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\012+\012+def set_token(home: Path | str, owner: str, token: str, *, expires: str | None = None, api_base: str = API_BASE,\012+              now: datetime | None = None) -> str:\012+    """Install the owner's read token: shape-check it, check it with a host GET ``/repos/<the owner's repo>`` (it must\012+    answer 200), take the expiry from GitHub's response header (else ``expires``; neither is a refusal), and write the\012+    record 0600. it had read the inbox at 12:4x and the watcher had no inbox\012+    trigger). The unread ids and count it has already told the desk about are persisted like NpWatch's cursor: `check()` holds\012+    the current set PENDING and the watcher calls `commit()` only after the wake is on the pipe. A message past the hub's first\012+    page (50, oldest first) shows as a higher unread COUNT. No seen file starts at what is unread now (history is the desk's own\012+    read). The wake names the count and plain-identifier sources, never content: an inbox message can carry an untrusted\012+    sender's words. An inbox unreadable for INBOX_BLIND_SECS (first failure persisted, cleared by a good read) is itself the\012+    trigger, because an unreadable inbox is a watcher blind to the very thing it was added to see."""\012+\012+    def __init__(self, fetch=None, seen: Path | None = None, fail: Path | None = None, clock=None):\012+        self._fetch, self._seen, self._fail, self._clock, self._pending = fetch, seen, fail, clock or time.time, None\012+\012+    def check(self) -> list[str]:\012+        fail_path = self._fail or inbox_fail_path()\012+        try:\012+            msgs, count = (self._fetch or _inbox_unread)()\012+        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - InboxError, a timeout, a refused connection: all the same blindness\012+            self._pending = None\012+            now = self._clock()\012+            got = _load_json(fail_path, None)\012+            since = got.get("since") if isinstance(got, dict) else None\012+            if not isinstance(since, (int, float)) or isinstance(since, bool) or since > now:\012+                _store_json(fail_path, {"since": now})\012+            elif now - since >= INBOX_BLIND_SECS:\012+                _store_json(fail_path, {"since": now})      # re-arm: the alert repeats every INBOX_BLIND_SECS, not on every poll\012+                raise CheckFailed(f"the inbox has been unreadable for {int((now - since) // 60)}m ({type(exc).__name__}): "\012+                                  "nothing wakes the desk on a new message") from exc\012+            return []\012+        try:\012+            fail_path.unlink()\012+        except OSError:      # absent is the usual case; any other failure leaves a stale clock that the next good read retries\012+            pass\012+        msgs = [m for m in msgs if isinstance(m, dict) and m.get("id")]\012+        ids = sorted(str(m["id"]) for m in msgs)\012+        self._pending = {"ids": ids, "count": int(count)}\012+        got = _load_json(self._seen or inbox_seen_path(), None)\012+        if got is None:\012+            return []\012+        known = set(got.get("ids") or []) if isinstance(got, dict) else set()\012+        prev = got.get("count") if isinstance(got, dict) else None\012+        new = [m for m in msgs if str(m["id"]) not in known]\012+        grew = len(ids) >= INBOX_PAGE and isinstance(prev, int) and not isinstance(prev, bool) and int(count) > prev\012+        if not new and not grew:\012+            return []\012+        n = max(len(new), int(count) - prev) if grew else len(new)\012+        sources = sorted({_source_word(m.get("source")) for m in new}) or ["unknown"]\012+        return [f"{n} new inbox message{'' if n == 1 else 's'} for {INBOX_READER} (from {', '.join(sources[:4])}): read it, `inbox.py unread --reader cli`"]\012+\012+    def commit(self) -> None:\012+        if self._pending is not None:\012+            _store_json(self._seen or inbox_seen_path(), self._pending)\012+            self._pending = None\012+\012+\012 def offset_for_since(since_ts: float, path: Path | None = None) -> int:\012     """The byte offset of the first complete line stamped at or after `since_ts`. The failures are the two a reader cannot tell from an empty board:\012+# a hold on Phill with no band row behind it (STALE), and a row declared ready that is not well-formed (needs grooming).\012+# One function grades a board, so every caller reads the same verdict (the callers are found by grepping `audit_failure`).\012+\012+def audit(repo: str | None = None) -> dict:\012+    """{repos: {repo: {graded, rows: [...], unreadable}}, failed: bool}. `rows` are the repo's STALE holds and its ungroomed\012+    declared-ready rows, each {id, kind, why, title}. An\012+    unreadable source is carried in `unreadable` and never counted as a clean board."""\012+    res = collect(all_categories=True, repo=repo)\012+    repos = sorted({i["repo"] for i in res["items"]} | ({repo} if repo else set(BOARD_FILES)))      # a graded board with no row read is still audited\012+    out: dict = {}\012+    for r in repos:\012+        mine = [i for i in res["items"] if i["repo"] == r]\012+        graded = r in BOARD_FILES\012+        rows = []\012+        for it in mine:\012+            if it.get("hold_stale"):\012+                rows.append({"id": it["id"], "kind": "stale-hold", "why": it["held_reason"], "title": it["title"]})\012+            elif it["declared_category"] == "ready" and it["needs_grooming"]:\012+                rows.append({"id": it["id"], "kind": "not-well-formed", "why": it["held_reason"] or "needs grooming",\012+                             "title": it["title"]})\012+        out[r] = {"graded": graded, "open_rows": len([i for i in mine if i["status_category"] not in ("done",)]),\012+                  "rows": rows if graded else [], "unreadable": list(res["unreadable"]) if graded else []}\012+    failed = any(v["graded"] and v["rows"] for v in out.values())\012+    return {"repos": out, "failed": failed}\012+\012+\012+def audit_failure(repo: str) -> list[dict] | None:\012+    """The repo's failing board rows for a gate to print; [] when the board is clean or the repo has no BOARD_FILES entry (no\012+    collect is paid for it); None when no clean verdict is possible: the audit raised, or a source the board is built from was\012+    unreadable and no row failed (an unreadable board is never a clean one).
